# Decision Log

> **Status:** Active — append-only  
> **Last updated:** 2026-10-24

ADR にするほどではないが、再計測や同じ失敗を避けるために残すべき決定と実測事実のログです。ADR を置き換えるものではありません。

## 運用ルール

- 新しい行をテーブルの先頭に追加します。初期エントリを除き、過去の決定を遡って追加しません。
- ADR がある決定は `Why` から ADR にリンクします。記入は機械検査しません。強制して中身のない行を増やすより、再判断に必要な根拠を残すことを優先します。
- 上書きされた決定は削除しません。新しい行を上に追加し、古い行の `Why` に新しい行への参照と、元の判断理由および変更理由を残します。
- リリースごとにそのリリース分を `docs/decisions/v0.3x.md` へアーカイブし、このファイルには常に直近 1 リリース分だけを残します。
- 決定日が確認できない初期エントリには確認日 `2026-09-08` を使い、`Why` に確認日であることを明記します。

| Date | Decision | Why |
|---|---|---|
| 2026-10-25 | UI の配置は Apple HIG に従う。新しい / 移動するコントロールは実装前に「HIG の原文引用 + 現状と提案を同縮尺で描いた図」の設計レビューを issue に、表示を変える PR は実機スクリーンショットの独立 HIG レビューを PR に載せる。HIG の P1 / P2 が開いている間は merge しない(Codex の有無に関係なく)。手順は `.claude/skills/hig-review/SKILL.md` | オーナー指示(#1764: conflict の「中止」がメインツールバー中央・ターミナルの隣・主操作に隣接して描かれていた。「わけわからん場所にボタンを作るな」)。個別の判断に任せると置き場所の誤りがテストを通って merge されるため、設計時と merge 前の 2 点で独立にゲートする。 |
| 2026-10-24 | #1742 extends existing diff preparation/rendering owners; preparation consolidation does not change media policy | Backend `file_reads` owns bounded frozen-side bytes; `main_diff_pane/preparation.rs` owns the shared off-thread entry; diff helpers own presentation, while conflict alone owns decisions/Save. Host-owned accepted pairs replace the raw cache. Step ① retains uncapped magic-only diff images and extension-first/BMP 8 MiB conflict images. Decide shared 8 MiB image input and magic-first PNG/JPEG/GIF/WebP/BMP for step ②; PDF remains 10 MiB. Proposed SidePair/SideContent, conflict PDF/actions and deletion acceptance are later steps, not completed features. [ADR-0228](adr/0228-shared-side-pair-viewer.md). |
| 2026-10-24 | #1739: both left sidebars use `PanelMotions::left` and shared reveal clipping | The dense conflict navigator previously unmounted immediately while the main navigator already followed #950/#955. Extend the existing lifecycle-owned pane motion, retaining its duration/easing and End anchor (reference: existing main sidebar slide toward its toggle), natural content width and unchanged row geometry. Toggle selection and focus handoff remain immediate; effective OS/app Reduce Motion settles through `motion::instant()` ([ADR-0173](adr/0173-reduce-motion.md)). No new controls or localized text. |
| 2026-10-24 | Saved conflict-drill projections constrain observable lifecycle behavior, not missing internal interleavings | Replay 34 projected traces and a forged unarmed-Skip negative control without shipping raw stderr. Changed-revision refresh disarms Skip and unreadable metadata refuses admission; retain the narrower post-confirm race as model-only. Keep independent witness/log lanes separate and preserve all visibility gaps. Generate TLC trace modules/results only under ignored output paths; retain the original projected evidence receipt. [Trace reconciliation](formal/conflict-trace-ledger.md). |
| 2026-10-24 | Conflict formal models are advisory regression guards; expectations change only with the corresponding fixes | Preserve the hand-extracted lifecycle/pure models and reconciled runtime ledgers under one pinned, self-checking harness. Expected REFUTATIONS track #1731–#1733, #1740, #1743–#1746 (model-external runtime findings: #1734–#1738) and are green only with witnesses; the expected-outcome Issue columns distinguish product holes, #1746 owner questions, deliberate negative controls, and C5's runtime metadata CONTRACT. A model fix makes the old expectation red, then the same fix PR updates it to HOLD. Every deliberate broken variant must remain red. These finite models do not prove Rust/model equivalence or runtime reachability. See [formal/conflict](../formal/conflict/README.md). |
| 2026-10-24 | Accent-filled component indicators use the Finder white-preferred 3:1 rule | Checkbox/radio marks are UI components, not 4.5:1 body-text labels; reuse `on_accent_foreground` as Apple primary buttons do, retaining black only when white falls below 3:1. Shared switch/slider thumbs, sidebar primary foreground and info foreground intentionally follow the same token. |
| 2026-10-19 | #1672: Mermaid separates logical image size from generated pixel density | Follow [HIG Images](https://developer.apple.com/design/human-interface-guidelines/images) and the existing PDF unit separation: raster density is UI zoom × window backing scale; generated pixels convert back to zoomed logical size. Reuse the bounded absolute-image/Contain layout to preserve aspect ratio under pane-width limits. Read PNG dimensions only on the worker; cache source/theme/density and share immutable paths. The existing Editor owner runs one CLI conversion at a time, then prepares the latest visible draft/scale; an old completion remains under its own key. No renderer service, fork or image-network-policy change. |
| 2026-10-19 | #1670: Mermaid failure notes show a bounded diagnostic headline, not stderr's last line | Real `mmdc` output ended in blank lines after its Chrome-missing explanation and stack trace, leaving an empty UI reason. Keep the first nonempty line through the existing 4 KiB/control-byte display bound; absent details get an explicit EN/JA fallback. Reuse the existing inline code card, secondary body-text role and constrained wrapping. No new modal, automatic install, retry policy or renderer. [HIG error guidance](https://developer.apple.com/design/human-interface-guidelines/alerts) favors specific context without unnecessary interruption. |
| 2026-10-19 | #1666: Editor placeholder explanations use secondary body text and bounded wrapping | Size-limit, binary and decoding explanations are required content, not disabled controls ([ADR-0125](adr/0125-apple-hig-themes.md)). Reuse `text_sub` without changing the palette. The existing centered helper constrains its text child to the pane, following the PDF status pattern: a real 940×660 / JA /167% window previously painted the size-limit sentence over the file tree. Preserve wording, file classification, selection and Git state. |
| 2026-10-17 | #1633: single-reference destructive confirmation actions explicitly count one branch | #454 requires counted footer actions as well as named targets. Preserve the short heading/chip design from #994 and existing two-stage approval; add the single affected branch count to Reset, local branch deletion and force-with-lease labels in EN/JA, including armed labels. Real Apple Light/EN/100% and Apple Dark/JA/167% windows at 1000×700 keep all six footer states visible; arm then cancel leaves local and remote fixture refs unchanged. |
| 2026-10-17 | #1633: shared plan warnings/blockers scroll as bounded sibling panels, or defer to an already-scrolling body | The explicit 200-note native case reached only 8 notes despite a contained card and pinned actions. Reuse the shell's viewport/compact ceiling; keep mandatory notes expanded and target panels independent. Structured note paths become plain content inside that same owner, eliminating nested scrolling. Test-only viewport measurements use `measure_leaf`, since an absolute child probe follows the content offset and can misdirect subsequent wheel input. No approval, execution, wording or palette change. |
| 2026-10-16 | #1640: compact membership uses configured rem × current root rem, not fixed 1000px | Default 70rem, integer 40–160 in Appearance, flat-string persistence through the existing store and typed hot reads. Logical coordinates already account for OS scale. Live threshold/zoom changes retain the existing compact lifecycle and session/input owners; desktop-only stress fixtures preserve narrow geometry with an isolated supported 40rem setting where needed. Supersedes the fixed breakpoint in [ADR-0224](adr/0224-compact-layout.md). |
| 2026-10-16 | Compact Commit popup hugs short drafts and keeps an independent height ceiling | A real 1080px compact window left about 90px below Commit with an empty body. Remove compact-only full-height constraints; measure painted popup height solely for existing anchored flip/clamp placement, without duplicating input sizing or cloning drafts. Short→long→short must release height again; [ADR-0224](adr/0224-compact-layout.md#pinned-wip-composer). |
| 2026-10-16 | #1617 Changes/Inspector: selection-fade text uses contrast-safe transient ink, not RGB interpolation | Actual active-window frame38 and native glyph pixels exposed 1.943:1 gray-on-blue text. Preserve exact wash0/1 label styles; choose one opaque primary/status/diffstat foreground against the composited in-flight wash using the existing on-accent3:1 policy. Logical selection and Git bar colours are unchanged. [ADR-0225](adr/0225-motion-system.md) |
| 2026-10-16 | #1617 Changes/Inspector/modal motion extends existing view owners without delaying intent | Stable file-path transitions paint selection wash; bounded fixed-slot `RowSplice` batches retain inert closing ghosts for status and Generated/Agent-artifacts folds. Supporting modal and command disclosures use measured reversible `Reveal`, reset with modal lifetime; destructive target lists remain non-collapsible. Initial/display-mode/large-list changes and Reduce Motion settle immediately. [ADR-0225 amendment](adr/0225-motion-system.md#changes-inspector-and-operation-disclosure-amendment-1617). |
| 2026-10-16 | #1617 Changes/Inspector: real translated rows own activation and default focus without broad occlusion | Child actions run before the role-less parent click/right-menu boundary. Changes resets a navigation-owner-local LeftDown claim in list capture, separately from child Button default prevention, preserving inline Stage/Unstage file focus while rejecting covered rows. A nonfocusable header/directory/More retains its child policy or existing ancestor root target. Keep mouse-down propagation, hover, drag, folder drops, wheel and child AX roles intact. Native controls check actual focus, AX row identity, keyboard/right-menu targets, real Stage/Unstage index effects and external drops. Path-derived IDs are cached on logical rebuilds. [ADR-0225](adr/0225-motion-system.md#inspector-generated-viewport-realization) |
| 2026-10-16 | #1617 Changes removal overlays consume context presses without broad occlusion | Native filename ink and paint order prove the visible top-file ghost before its right press; consume RightMouseDown only on that exit-row host so a covered live file cannot open its menu. Left input, focus/AX and same-point ancestor repository drops remain unchanged. Inspector's ghost-after-header negative control needs no additional handler. Native drop controls establish pointer modality after Escape and prove the menu has actually repainted closed; they do not claim an OS Finder gesture. [ADR-0225](adr/0225-motion-system.md#inspector-generated-viewport-realization) |
| 2026-10-16 | #1617 a closing Graph WIP ghost consumes only right-button presses | A native 37ms overlap test proved that the first-prefix ghost, painted after the surviving commit, opened that covered commit's menu. Stop only Right MouseDown on ghosts; retain normal hitboxes and ancestor external drops. Current WIP's genuinely visible leading strip has no underlying row and needs no new handler. Native controls preserve commit, stash and linked-worktree menus at 100% and 167% zoom. |
| 2026-10-16 | #1617 Graph mode return shares the root's actual-center visibility edge | Tab activation alone misses rapid PR/Issues/Editor returns. Mark the previous visible CommitList owner's existing reset flag and consume Graph synchronization only when its CommitList is actually visible. Hidden reads cannot consume the reset or replay arrivals; the first returning frame settles once, while later live changes still animate. Reuse the cached owner/center edge rather than scan sessions or duplicate workspace routing. [ADR-0225](adr/0225-motion-system.md#graph-retained-visibility-boundary). |
| 2026-10-16 | #1617 Graph follow-up: real animated rows own overlapping clicks; viewport fringe preserves logical paint order | A four-commit insertion at 37ms painted the expected row but selected an overlapping predecessor. Stop propagation only in real commit/WIP/stash click handlers, preserving ancestor hover, external folder drops, mouse-down focus and wheel chaining; ghosts and fixed shells remain inert. Mount leading fringe before the first requested row and trailing fringe after the last. Native CommitId clicks cover compressed rows and realized tails at 100%/167% zoom; occupied-row drops and stash focus from an Input guard ancestor behavior. |
| 2026-10-16 | #1621 compressed rows own pointer semantics at the translated real-content boundary, not the final fixed slot | Frozen native input proved covered siblings replace Editor file/directory selection and Navigator branch/head/tag/stash/worktree targets, while a nonfocusable subgroup borrows the covered ref's right menu. Consume only Click / otherwise-unhandled Right at the real row, preserving existing under_ring focus, child links, compact pointer worktree selection+dismissal, fixed-slot keyboard actions and ancestor drop. A later-painted top-removal ghost separately claims Right only; visible fringes retain measured nonoverlap rather than a generic blocker. [ADR-0225](adr/0225-motion-system.md#pointer-ownership-during-row-compression-1621). |
| 2026-10-16 | #1621 retained Editor motion retires on actual workspace visibility edges, not only repository-tab activation | PRs/Issues keep the Editor entity alive, so a rapid return otherwise resumes its old wash or ghosts. Share the root's existing resolved center before Home/conflict early returns and cache only the last presented owner/center. Retire the outgoing Editor once; unchanged frames do no owner scan, clone/update or allocation. Optional owner lookup also handles last-tab removal. Both tree sources retain frozen selection/collapse return and resumed-motion coverage. [ADR-0225](adr/0225-motion-system.md#review-corrections). |
| 2026-10-16 | #1621 presentation-only Editor tree rows consume context-menu presses instead of bubbling to the root | Removing file/directory handlers does not make ghosts or displaced fringes inert when the enclosing blank tree opens a creation menu. Stop right-button propagation in their shared presentation wrapper; retain ordinary file, directory and actual blank-space targets. Frozen-clock native regression checks both tree sources at a visible closing ghost. [ADR-0225](adr/0225-motion-system.md#review-corrections). |
| 2026-10-16 | #1621 all-collapsed pane reveal measures the existing flex viewport, not occupied header heights | The collapsed stack leaves unused container space; summing its children gives a zero natural body and an endpoint jump. Record the real pane viewport with the existing canvas measurement convention and subtract measured divider gaps. Frozen-clock native coverage includes midpoint, endpoint continuity, closed resizing and Reduce Motion. |
| 2026-10-16 | #1621 compact rail replacement retires each skipped Git pane's ring, even while the rail remains front | Whole-sidebar `front=false` is insufficient for a compact A → B → A switch: only B's row processor runs. Clear skipped pane transitions with its list; PR/Issues popovers have no Git pane and clear all Git rings before focus evacuation. Frozen-clock compact return coverage retains the original native unfocused pixels. |
| 2026-10-16 | #1621: whole-Navigator focus evacuation clears retained ring presentation immediately | Hidden and replaced panes have no virtual-list processor to retarget the old ring. Clear its identity and transitions at the existing `sync_sidebar_focus(front=false)` boundary, not on the first returning row paint. Native frozen-clock View-hide / PR / Issues / Editor return coverage checks logical focus and actual ring pixels. [ADR-0225](adr/0225-motion-system.md#review-corrections). |
| 2026-10-16 | #1617 Editor crossfade must preserve the existing foreground endpoints; contrast adjustment is transition-only | Apple selection uses the existing on-accent policy (including its 3:1 threshold), not whichever color maximizes contrast. A max-contrast rule changed settled blue/white filenames to blue/black. Frozen-clock endpoint parity follows the prior theme policy; actual mid-flight appearance needs Metal pixel evidence, not assertions that repeat the compositing formula. |
| 2026-10-16 | #1617 tree review correction: animate the existing Navigator focus ring, never a new selected-looking fill | The baseline Navigator had only a keyboard focus ring and pointer hover, not a persistent focused-row wash. Preserve that visual contract and AX state. Editor foreground follows composited selection color; displaced tree rows include inert viewport fringes using actual measured list pitch. Keep initial-read readiness until the first accepted repository read, not its loading headers. [ADR-0225](adr/0225-motion-system.md#sidebar-and-editor-tree-amendment-1617). |
| 2026-10-16 | #1617 sidebar/editor trees extend their existing presentation owners, not their logical row models | Keep fixed virtual row slots and immediate keyboard/AX visibility. Stable ref/worktree/stash/path identities drive selection washes and `RowSplice`; outgoing children are presentation-only ghosts. Sidebar pane bodies use `Reveal`, while disclosure chevrons change immediately to communicate the logical state. Initial/replacement lists do not play insertion motion. [ADR-0225](adr/0225-motion-system.md#sidebar-and-editor-tree-amendment-1617). |
| 2026-10-16 | #1617 Graph follow-up: real animated rows own overlapping pointer hits; viewport fringe preserves logical paint order | A four-commit insertion at 37ms painted the expected row but selected an overlapping predecessor. Use public `block_mouse_except_scroll` only on interactive commit/WIP/stash rows, never ghosts or fixed shells; keep wheel chaining. Mount leading fringe before the first requested row and trailing fringe after the last. Native CommitId clicks cover compressed rows and realized tails at 100%/167% zoom. |
| 2026-10-16 | #1617 Graph: session-owned keyed selection and fixed-slot row-content splices; preserve scrolled reading anchors | Extend existing Graph renderers and `TabUiState`, using shared motion primitives only. Commit arrivals require an accepted full read; WIP membership follows cheap refreshes. Initial/activation/filter/paging/large changes settle. Above-viewport splices preserve the first visible identity and pixel offset and settle; visible top arrivals animate complete row content and measured lane canvases. [ADR-0225 Graph amendment](adr/0225-motion-system.md#graph-surface-amendment-1617). |
| 2026-10-16 | #1617 review: one `RowSplice<K>` handles batched insertion and removal, including fixed-height tree collapse | Track only active logical keys and their current presence; insertions subtract absent slot height, removals add retained ghost height. Reversing membership changes the final-list baseline but preserves painted positions and opacity. Callers retain ghost content/order and supply the nearest surviving anchor; no indices are stored. Replaces the single-insertion primitive rather than adding another convention. [ADR-0225](adr/0225-motion-system.md). |
| 2026-10-16 | #1617 baseline repair: right-selection motion's full-width oracle allows the same 1px tolerance as its reversal checks | Before the motion extraction, clean `origin/dev` at `de19a71dd827b2c1a0fa80b6ce79a4afa7c5e2dc` reproduced `Some(327.5) != Some(327.6)` at `side_panel_motion.rs:387`: native device-pixel-snapped bounds were compared exactly to unsnapped theme geometry. Apply that tolerance only to the five full-width assertions; missing/closing clips, selection, reversal and instant-path coverage remain. No production behavior change. |
| 2026-10-16 | #1617: extend pane motion into `kagi_ui_core::motion`, with one clock, Reduce Motion gate and view-owned frame scheduling; no dependency fork | Owner selects approach A: retain Graph's fixed-height `uniform_list`, offset rows below each inserted logical key and fade in that key while the final list length updates immediately. Use 150–250ms one-shots and CommitId/worktree-path/file-path identity, never indices. GUI E2E stays instant unless its shared clock is frozen. This foundation preserves user-visible pane behavior; selection/insertion/accordion surfaces are separate slices. [ADR-0225](adr/0225-motion-system.md). |
| 2026-10-16 | #1614 re-review: asynchronous restart admission preserves the current modal | Installer completion records installed/restart-pending state, clears only its own Update modal and offers the restart guard through asynchronous modal arbitration. An unrelated plan, editor prompt or dirty-buffer intent wins; completion never replaces it or exits behind it. The user can later open Update and explicitly restart. Recovery guidance follows the host-blocking non-running lease, not a lease-free recovery requirement from another repository; running writes and clones continue to receive waiting guidance. |
| 2026-10-16 | #1614 review: installed updates retain an explicit manual Restart retry | Cancel retires the current relaunch intent but keeps the installed replacement and restart-pending state; reopening Update offers Restart, never another install, and disables Skip. A clean editor blocked by an operation gets Restart / Cancel, not a zero-unsaved-file Save All / Discard dialog. Running work says to wait and select Restart again; an Unknown retained lease instead says to Cancel and review recovery in Operation Log. No completion callback automatically restarts the app: the user explicitly retries after work/recovery, avoiding a surprise restart after returning to editing. [ADR-0197 follow-up](adr/0197-session-owned-ui-state.md#host-close-follow-up-1614). |
| 2026-10-15 | #1614: retain only native application termination admission as upstream candidate U2 | Read-only audit of pinned `gpui 0.2.2` at `caf5007618c309a8ea66002d44a9c9ab629a2d35`, official crates.io `gpui 0.2.2`, and Kit-adopted `gpui-pre` / `gpui-pre-macos 0.3.9` found no public `applicationShouldTerminate:` veto/deferred-reply API. `on_app_quit` explicitly cannot cancel; its bounded cleanup is not an unsaved-file confirmation. The snapshot's platform `on_quit -> bool` handles AppCell borrow status and macOS ignores that result at `applicationWillTerminate:`. Dock Quit / normal logout / shutdown therefore remain a gap distinct from Kagi-owned shared host-close routes. After those routes pass acceptance, #1614 stays open **only for U2**. Repository owner publishes the prepared English upstream PR/Discussion and records its URL/adoption revision; close/remove the gap only after official public admission/deferral integrates with the existing guard and isolated native acceptance covers Cancel, failed Save, busy hold and successful termination. No fork, dependency edit or delegate-replacement shim; not a new #1479-S6 prerequisite. [Versioned source paths, evidence and unpublished owner proposal](rearch/upstream-migration-1479.md#native-termination-u2). No checks or OS-termination runtime validation performed for this audit. |
| 2026-10-15 | #1604/#1608, Refs #1607: native menu state and recent history share existing owners; Find stays within public APIs | Plain names plus native checked state replace text prefixes; active workspace uses noun labels. File/Dock recent entries use the Home history store and existing repository open path. Owner approved Find… only: private pinned Kit navigation cannot support Next/Previous/Use Selection without a library change, so omit those commands and propose official public actions. Cmd-F retains Input search priority, then the existing non-Input/non-Terminal Graph sidebar filter. [ADR-0085](adr/0085-unified-menu-definition.md). |
| 2026-10-15 | #1604/#1608 menu review: native availability follows actual dispatch targets | Register Kagi Find only for a searchable focused Input or the existing Graph sidebar target, forwarding Kit's public Search without stealing Input Cmd-F. Register Clear Menu on main/Home/Settings roots only with nonempty history; AppKit ignores disabled metadata and otherwise sees unconditional global handlers as enabled. Dedicated EN/JA workspace menu nouns avoid toolbar abbreviations. Portable Open Recent retains a heading, surrounding/internal separators and the shared check column. |
| 2026-10-15 | #1608 Dock review: recent repositories remain actionable without a window | Dock entries contain only OpenRecentRepository actions, never Clear Menu or separators. File > Open Recent retains its history-only Clear Menu. A global recent-repository action opens the existing repository path in a new host when all windows are closed; hidden native coverage exercises the production host creation without taking foreground. |
| 2026-10-15 | #1608 recent-open activation: preserve the last main-window target across utility windows | WindowMenus remembers its last active main host independently of the utility menu owner. Open Recent prefers the active main host, then that remembered host, then native activation order. Activate that host and the app before the open attempt, including newly created hosts, so a deleted repository's existing error toast/footer is visible too. Hidden native coverage checks repository routing, rendered failure feedback and requested activation without bringing windows onscreen; OS foreground assertions remain Tier B. |
| 2026-10-15 | #1606 / #1609: own Kagi-rendered text menus and preserve child-first cancellation | The public `Input::context_menu` builder replaces menus on Kagi-rendered Inputs; defer until InputState's right-click lease ends, then resolve existing `Msg` labels and current selection/clipboard/disabled availability. Component-owned Find/Replace Inputs retain gpui-kit’s English menu: SearchPanel has no public builder/locale forwarding API, and fork/pin changes are forbidden. Track that upstream API candidate in the migration plan. Command-Period clones registered Escape actions and predicates through GPUI's public keymap API, including component-private action types without importing them. Unbind only the Input ToggleCodeActions candidate so unconsumed Input Escape can continue to the next child-owner cancellation binding; never redispatch workspace cancellation from an Input Escape listener. Keep the same positive KagiWorkspace fallback as Escape; native Settings alerts have an explicit dialog context because their existing Escape handler is a raw key listener. [ADR-0048](adr/0048-ui-localization.md). |
| 2026-10-15 | #1479-S1b review: style independent tooltip and branch-drag roots explicitly | Correct the initial acceptance scope below: GPUI prepaints `HoverHelp` and `BranchDragGhost` outside Kit Root. Both now apply `ui_font()` themselves. A native child-prepaint probe reproduced missing fallbacks and non-JP Han FontIds in system-Noto × JA before the fix; the expanded Linux matrix compares each actual inherited overlay font with the bundled JP reference. Other drag views are textless dividers; deferred/anchored descendants retain GPUI's saved text-style stack. Keep the registry cutover and upstream #522 recommendation. [ADR-0130](adr/0130-bundled-japanese-font-fallback.md#correction-detached-tooltip-and-branch-drag-roots-1479-s1b-review). |
| 2026-10-15 | #1479-S1b: remove the cosmic-text patch after native Linux acceptance | Supersedes the S1 removal deferral below: registry 0.19.0 passed the independent JA/EN × minimal/system-Noto × X11/Wayland native matrix, with the production Kagi terminal/diff/composer and an explicit JP reference/TextView in one fixture window. The existing real CosmicTextSystem tests retain exact face/weight and Nerd-icon oracles. Xvfb's blank Vulkan presentation persisted with working Mesa lavapipe; disabling Vulkan ICD discovery selected working llvmpipe OpenGL. Keep upstream PR #522 open for implicit-fallback consumers; Kagi no longer depends on its merge. [ADR-0130](adr/0130-bundled-japanese-font-fallback.md#amendment-2026-10-15-registry-cosmic-text-after-linux-acceptance-1479-s1b). |
| 2026-10-15 | #1605 review: folder-drop feedback uses the receiving hitbox and invalidates on drag exit | Share the repository root's named hover group with the out-of-flow feedback frame; directory-only `can_drop` controls feedback without accepting files. Observe public `FileDropEvent::Exited` through a passive canvas mouse listener and refresh the window so cancelled/departed drags cannot leave stale feedback. Native pixel regressions cover exit without forced refresh and modal occlusion. No dependency or fork changes. |
| 2026-10-15 | #1605: route external folder drops and macOS folder-open requests through existing repository-tab opening | Finder/window drops and Dock/open-file requests share the existing repository-opening and single-instance paths rather than a second tab/session owner. Highlight the window drop target, reject files, and retain existing non-repository failure feedback and klog contracts. Register `public.folder` in the generated macOS `CFBundleDocumentTypes` with role `Viewer` and `LSHandlerRank` `Alternate`, so accepting Dock drops does not claim the default folder handler. No dependency or fork changes. |
| 2026-10-15 | #1603: host close uses one aggregate unsaved-file confirmation | macOS-style Save All / Discard / Cancel covers every retained repository editor and its background file tabs, with a localized count. Save All serializes existing conflict-aware writes and only continues after all buffers are clean; failure leaves the guard open, Cancel retires the exit intent, and Discard does not write files. Lease/clone admission is checked before the guard and again at exit. Settings Quit raises the owning repository's guard rather than bypassing it. [ADR-0197 amendment](adr/0197-session-owned-ui-state.md#host-close-amendment-1603). |
| 2026-10-15 | #1588 SHARED: translate known legacy diagnostics and receipt summaries only at the presentation boundary | Operation identifiers, StateSummary and persisted errors retain their existing bytes. Known-format adapters produce EN/JA previews without word-sanitizing user names or paths. Dedicated plan-note contract rendering bypasses display payload projection for stderr arguments. UI-only prompts and notices use display formatters directly; shared receipt text is normalized by the existing modal/footer presenters. Operation Log summaries are localized; Details/Copy retain complete recorded causes and full backup identifiers needed for command-line recovery. Guidance must not imply unsupported GUI restoration or entry deletion. Separate contract formatters preserve durable receipt construction; no outcome or safety decision may depend on translated text. |
| 2026-10-15 | #1588: keep user-facing EN/JA copy separate from persisted diagnostic rendering | Describe what happened or what the user can do, not pipeline phases, ADRs, internal identifiers or storage mechanisms. Preserve `message_en`/receipt and klog contracts; display-only plan renderers own revised English while existing presenters own Japanese. Extend the existing uv Rule/literal-selector infrastructure with `check-ui-internal-jargon` for bilingual i18n and domain display text, excluding comments and legacy diagnostic renderers. |
| 2026-10-15 | #1591 review: activation is checked at delayed creation, not only observed afterward | Initial inactive state cancels help; ordinary workspace tests cover the pure cancellation predicate and TestWindow covers deactivation during the delay. Forward layout/paint without extra children or icon-button sizing changes; preserve Kit content/style and interactive worktree-card ownership. Native baseline reproduced removal persistence, and TestWindow exposed GPUI's retained bounds snapshot. No dependency/fork/pin changes. Retire this boundary only when official managed tooltips provide equivalent lifetime/geometry/activation guarantees and Kagi's regression coverage passes at the adopted revision (#1479-S6). Not runtime-tested upstream. | 2026-10-12 | #1264: historical snapshots own a selectable TextView, never an editable InputState | The pinned Input has no read-only-but-selectable contract; disabled loses selection/focus. CRLF uses one block to preserve clipboard separators, with no bounded-layout claim. Only foreground Editor historical state blocks Save/Undo/Redo; a retained Editor behind PRs must not suppress root Undo (native modal assertion). | 2026-10-11 | Status-bar Terminal/Operation Log reuse the header's named `keyboard_nav::focusable` icon-button contract | #1207. Dense footer controls keep 22-reference-px height and 16px glyphs; neutral `surface` hover and the existing fixed accent ring identify interaction. Stable localized AX names accompany state-dependent Show/Hide tooltips; `aria_toggled` is the pinned GPUI/AccessKit pressed-state API for Button (there is no `aria_pressed`). Terminal retains Cmd+J; Operation Log has no dedicated shortcut, so its tooltip identifies focused Enter/Space rather than inventing a binding. Keyboard Terminal activation retains the button focus; pointer activation keeps PTY focus. Existing callbacks and klog stay unchanged. [ADR-0125 amendment](adr/0125-apple-hig-themes.md). |
| 2026-10-11 | #1217 review preserves ADR-0048 English domain vocabulary inside translated JA reasons; native overflow tests require a closed tooltip positive control and shrink an open menu to 480px | ASCII prohibition incorrectly rejected domain words. PopupMenu's width cap applies to inner items, so subtract its two outer 1px borders to keep the whole menu within the viewport minus 32px. [ADR-0125 amendment](adr/0125-apple-hig-themes.md). |
| 2026-10-11 | #1217 overflow rows reserve a nonshrinking first-line shortcut, with a shrinking/truncated secondary-reason label column; cap PopupMenu against the current viewport and suppress the open trigger tooltip | Reuse gpui-kit menu/navigation and action-bound Kbd, not a replacement menu. Reasons retain full AX descriptions and the existing refusal footer. JA toolbar guards use Japanese prose while English remains unchanged. [ADR-0125 amendment](adr/0125-apple-hig-themes.md). |
| 2026-10-11 | #1203/#1204 review: clear Home attribute observations per matrix cell, consume tooltip observations after hover, and bundle AX description assignment with recording | Prevent a previous Light/EN/100% tooltip from falsely satisfying Dark/JA/167% assertions. The description helper applies `aria_description` and records the identical argument together; evidence proves the assignment path, not live AccessKit/VoiceOver readback. [ADR-0125 amendment](adr/0125-apple-hig-themes.md). |
| 2026-10-11 | Home repository rows retain whole-row keyboard activation; trailing Clone/Open uses `KagiButton` Secondary at 22 reference px with no extra Tab stop, while metadata retains `text_sub` on `surface` | #1203/#1204. Reuse gpui-kit Button's role/name, border, hover and pressed states rather than hand-painting a chip. Child clicks stop bubbling to avoid duplicate activation. Full sanitized description is converted to SharedString when cached list entries change, then shared by visible text, Tooltip and row AX description; truncation and virtual-list positions remain unchanged. Native scenario covers Apple Light/Dark, EN/JA and 100%/167%; real-window comparison remains integration-owner Tier B. [ADR-0125 amendment](adr/0125-apple-hig-themes.md). |
| 2026-10-11 | Bottom-panel pane selectors reuse `keyboard_nav::TabList` with manual activation and rounded cells similar to the sidebar selector; selection wins over hover | #1205 / #1206. Styling is analogous, not shared: bottom cells retain 28px height and `selected_for_window`, while sidebar cells use their existing fill policy. Terminal activation may start a process, so arrows move focus without selecting. Keyboard activation retains selector focus; pointer activation gives the PTY text input. Existing fixed ring, EN/JA names and Cmd+J remain. [ADR-0125 amendment](adr/0125-apple-hig-themes.md). |
| 2026-10-11 | #1208/#1209 review: derive filled danger label/hover/pressed exactly like Primary, attach Clone Escape to the outer tracked task frame and restore root focus on all clone cancellation paths, expose Remote Browse's available default action | Fixed-white danger labels measured only 2.32:1 on Mocha; the new all-preset regression requires ≥4.5:1 in rest/hover/pressed and distinct interaction fills, including Color Vision. An allocation-free outer-frame customization closure keeps Escape on the actual focus owner. Remote Connect/Open Repository custom actions follow the same busy/repository availability as their buttons. [ADR-0125 amendment](adr/0125-apple-hig-themes.md). |
| 2026-10-11 | Dialog Cancel maps to `ButtonRole::Secondary` / Default outline, default to Primary, destructive to filled Destructive; shared input frame applies existing `apply_dialog` and `focus_trap` without nesting plan boundaries | #1208/#1209. NSAlert's leading Cancel/trailing default hierarchy, S geometry and `colors.input` boundary are reused, not hand-painted. Named input boundaries added to Create Branch/Tag/Worktree, Stash Push, Rename Branch/Set Upstream, Worktree Lock Reason, Editor filesystem prompts, Clone, Remote Browse and PR Fields. Existing plan-card, Amend, Discard and Stash Apply boundaries keep Dialog/AlertDialog and confirm-stage actions. Existing Escape, marked-text handling and inert blocked buttons remain their owners. [ADR-0125 amendment](adr/0125-apple-hig-themes.md). |
| 2026-10-11 | #1210 toolbar overflow fits cached natural button widths against the toolbar element's own canvas layout bounds; repository commands collapse before workspace controls, selected mode last | Canvas prepaint records natural widths into a fixed-size, window-owned array keyed by presentation/language/zoom and requests a next frame only when a measurement changes. This preserves caption geometry and the centered wide layout without guessing text widths or reading window/E2E bounds. The row clips intermediate paint while the next-frame fit settles. Hidden controls omit the interactive subtree: GPUI still registers Tab stops on `display: none` elements. The named `keyboard_nav::focusable` trigger and gpui-kit Popover/PopupMenu share the ordinary activation owner; Kbd reads actual action bindings for shortcuts. The pinned Button cannot independently name an icon-only AX node, so the existing named header helper is used. Native count assertions remain unchanged for visible glyphs; hidden glyphs require actual menu reachability. [ADR-0125 amendment](adr/0125-apple-hig-themes.md). |
| 2026-10-11 | #1210 review: retain the last fit during remeasurement, gate gaps by visible neighbours, and explicitly dispose controlled overflow before removing its element | Hidden controls receive a noninteractive, invisible natural-width probe only while measurements are pending. This keeps the menu/trigger stable through style changes without leaving invisible Tab stops. Popup caches are keyed by hidden-mask/reason snapshots; dismissal restores prior focus only when focus still belongs to the menu, so Terminal keeps PTY focus. A strong popover-state handle permits synchronous dismissal and unregisters gpui-kit's deferred context before the trigger vanishes. Native coverage exercises language/zoom updates while open, Terminal focus, widening while keyboard-open, and the actual text-input right-click guard afterward. |
| 2026-10-11 | #1199/#1201 review: Radio dot uses `primary_foreground`, and shared production `measure_inside` placeholders are absolute zero-sized elements | Hard-coded white had insufficient contrast on Dark yellow and Mocha; bridge foreground preserves ≥4.5:1. Supersedes the white-only policy below. An in-flow empty probe changed `justify_between` and flex gaps in production. Unit regression asserts absolute position and zero size; native probe covers theme accent plus blue/purple System accents. [ADR-0125 amendment](adr/0125-apple-hig-themes.md). |
| 2026-10-11 | Settings structural hierarchy uses `text_sub` 12-reference-px semibold headings and literal 1px `surface` borders; selected Radio uses a white central dot, not a checkbox SVG | #1199 / #1201. Reuse #1177/#1175 roles and the existing gpui-kit RadioGroup. Fork `fix/radio-dot` starts at pinned `b50a079`; only selected-mark painting changes, preserving focus, pointer, group selection and AX. [ADR-0125 amendment](adr/0125-apple-hig-themes.md). |
| 2026-10-11 | #1202 review: Inspector/Activity handles live in TabUiState; Home Recent has an independent HomeGithub handle; Settings resets only on open; native presentation restores on Drop | Positional state must not leak between repository sessions. Audit includes Settings, all three Home GitHub panes, Recent, Inspector message/files, Activity ranking and existing Operation Log. Excluded: PR navigator/commit strip/file list and Conflict dashboard/context scrollers have distinct workspace owners; menu/popover/modal previews have transient focus/layout owners outside the named audit. Diff/editor/terminal retain their existing independent scrollbar rendering. This is not a repository-wide thumb claim. [ADR-0125](adr/0125-apple-hig-themes.md). |
| 2026-10-11 | Settings / Home lists / Inspector files and message / Activity ranking attach gpui-kit vertical thumbs to persistent owners; Scrolling overlays, Hover/Always reserve 16px and pin visibility | #1202, reuse #1182's system policy without a new setting. Home retains its ListState virtualization and keyboard row scroll. Kit ScrollbarState is private and retained by GPUI under a stable element id, not recreated in view render. Audit: Inspector's two bare overflow regions and Activity ranking were missing bars and are migrated; Operation Log already uses ListState plus with_vertical_scrollbar and is intentionally unchanged. No new user text, so EN/JA translations remain unchanged. [ADR-0125 amendment](adr/0125-apple-hig-themes.md). |
| 2026-10-11 | #1202 native mutation check removes only the scrollbar attachment, keeping the measured region and persistent owner: both Settings/Home thumb drags leave offset at zero and fail; restored attachment passes | Settings 940×660@100% measured body 797×512.5, extent 1877.3, thumb 139.912; drag changes Y 0→−1343.284. Final native matrix passes Settings/Home at 940×660, EN/JA, 100%/167%, Scrolling/Always, including repaint retention and Home refresh/language anchoring. Settings Switch spring callbacks are drained before unmount using the existing settings_switches teardown; leak assertions remain enabled. Thumb bounds are derived from measured host bounds and kit geometry, not direct native AX thumb bounds. Tier B remains main-agent-owned. |
| 2026-10-11 | Home's recent-repositories heading uses the #1177 section-header typography (12 reference-px semibold `text_sub`), without a disclosure chevron; bounded single-line text uses horizontal ellipsis and a full localized tooltip, with 8 reference-px vertical padding protecting descenders | #1197: Home is an open surface but its sidebar heading is the same secondary navigation role. Reuse existing GPUI truncation and Tooltip, not a second heading abstraction; keep scaled sidebar width and noninteractive heading semantics. Native coverage checks production width, fixed 300 and 220 logical-px sidebar overrides (gui-e2e only; 220px at 167% JA forces truncation), EN/JA at 100%/167% under Apple Light/Dark; width shaping uses the actual Inter UI font, and the painted line box fits its mask. [ADR-0125](adr/0125-apple-hig-themes.md#amendment--2026-10-11-quiet-graph-navigator-group-headers-1177). |
| 2026-10-11 | #1178 review: captions, AX names and default tooltips share localized short labels; toolbar presentation is a `MENU_BAR` dynamic submenu in both native and in-app View menus | JA English captions paired with translated AX names violated label-in-name. `DynSubmenu::ToolbarDisplay` and shared entry labels/markers avoid a macOS-only menu injection; both renderers dispatch `SetToolbarDisplay`. Native coverage asserts JA caption/name/tooltip equality and clicks both in-app items. [ADR-0125 amendment](adr/0125-apple-hig-themes.md). |
| 2026-10-11 | Toolbar display is a flat `toolbar_display` string (`icon_only` / `icon_and_text`); unset follows the active theme, Apple Light/Dark icon-only, others captions. Extend existing rendering, settings store, RadioGroup and native View menu; 36px compact row, 20px icons, unchanged 52px caption geometry. No exceptional captions: arrows indicate transfer, plus creates a branch, inbox/folder indicate stash storage/application, undo/redo arrows show history direction, terminal/window-pen/graph/PR/chart distinguish workspaces and analysis; localized tooltip and AX name disambiguate Git-specific operations. | 2026-10-11 | #1180 review: rail removal is Apple-only; Dark inactive tabs use `bg_base` rather than strip `panel`; worktree tab lane rank is removed | Non-Apple fill pairs include One Light 1.010:1, Flower Road 1.082:1, Tokyo Night key 1.099:1 and Latte non-key 1.142:1, so they retain their active accent rail and palette. Supersedes the all-theme rail-removal/panel-inactive policy immediately below. [ADR-0125 amendment](adr/0125-apple-hig-themes.md). |
| 2026-10-11 | Repository/Home document tabs use `bg_base` active / `surface` inactive in Apple Light and `surface` active / `panel` inactive in Apple Dark, regardless of key-window state; all themes remove top rails and worktree-wide tint, retaining the 🌲 marker | #1180. | 2026-10-11 | Graph navigator の top-level header は28 reference px（12px line＋上下8px）、secondary `text_sub` の semibold、localized title と独立した小さい chevron／neutral count pill にする。20／24px body slot は変えない | #1177。Unicode triangle＋英語大文字＋BOLD の一体文字列が console heading の印象を作っていた。五つの pinned header と collapse minimum だけ高さを分離し、既存 focus／AX／persist owner を維持する。[ADR-0125 amendment](adr/0125-apple-hig-themes.md)。 |
| 2026-10-11 | Pane divider paint is independent of its scaled 4px resize target: centered literal `px(1.)` in `surface`, adjacent `sidebar` / `panel` / `bg_base` fill, unchanged resize handlers | #1174 review: PR navigator uses `sidebar`, PR swimlane and Conflict use `bg_base`; bottom-panel and inspector-split boundaries also use hairlines. Group hover/drag-over makes the interaction accent solid across target and line. The container drag style checks the exact kind; pinned GPUI group_drag_over cannot inspect the payload kind, so a different-kind drag crossing another handle may transiently accent only its 1px line (accepted), not its whole target. Labels win; no unrelated palette change. [ADR-0125](adr/0125-apple-hig-themes.md). |

| 2026-10-11 | #1172: pinned gpui-kit の Input next-frame cleanup は WeakEntity を capture し、実行時に upgrade する。公開 wrap_row_count commit を継承した `b50a079693ee06084f47db29b570f189fd941fc4` に UI／assets を揃える | force_capture の構築 trace と EntityId 照合により漏れた8 entity は session-owned list filter と特定し、resize 中の再生成ではないと確認。restored lifecycle の teardown は各 filter の4 handle（計32）を報告した。強参照の owner は SDK `input/element.rs` の `window.on_next_frame` であり、Kagi の owner を二重化／早期破棄せず callback の寿命を弱参照に限定する。native MacWindow callback に pending frame が残っても input を延命しない。`pr_detail_lifecycle` は leak assertion を有効に保つ。[ADR-0197](adr/0197-session-owned-ui-state.md)。 |
| 2026-10-11 | Commit Panel は sidebar の実表示幅と divider を引いた viewport から logical 120px の graph 最小幅を予約し、persisted panel width を上限に使う。pane motion の clip と子 entity は同じ cap を使い、設定値は変更しない | #1171。scaled 固定幅だけでは狭幅・167%で右端が viewport を越える。dense file row は path を truncate し完全な repository-relative path を tooltip／AX に保持。既存 viewport matrix に long-path の List／Tree と EN／JA の右端測定、desktop 幅の equality を追加。 |
| 2026-10-11 | Commit Panel の file lists は section header の minimum を保持し、本文は fork の cached `wrap_row_count()` を3–12行に cap して preferred height に使う。action chrome を minimum に予約し、message/footer を clip して Commit への overpaint を防ぐ | #1167 review。AutoGrow と同じ soft-wrap 計測を allocation なしで再利用し、separator の空行を本文行数に数えない。fork rev `f7e696f6b057b2b091d9b95b5c12cc0f7dfc4f91`、3／11行と72-column wrap、940×560@167% の native 回帰を追加。[ADR-0134 amendment](adr/0134-simpler-commit-pane.md#amendment--2026-10-11-adaptive-composer-1167)。 |

| 2026-10-11 | #1118 review: width adaptation は detail のみ。drawer は center 内に置き、inline に戻った reveal は破棄する。Escape は実際に表示中の drawer だけ閉じ、PR 未選択の palette command は EN/JA の disabled reason を返す | Dashboard の Toggle Sidebar を奪わず、navigator／bottom panel を覆わず、resize 後の stale reveal による余分な Escape を防ぐ。native 回帰は desktop の既定 inline panes、swimlane keyboard reveal と state retention を検査する。Dashboard→detail→native resize→Home は origin/dev でも InputState leak するため別件として切り離す。[ADR-0200 amendment](adr/0200-pr-workspace-layout.md#1118-width-aware-detail-companions)。 |

| 2026-10-11 | theme bridge は `color_remote` から filled Info の fill／foreground／hover／active 全 family を導出し、Incoming pane／marker と側選択を一致させる。Primary と同じ contrast-preserving interaction 計算を共有する | #1078。generic `info` の指定だけでは pinned Button が読む `button_info*` に届かず、preset 色が漏れていた。success／danger tint と generic notice 色は変更しない。[ADR-0036](adr/0036-color-themes.md)。 |

| 2026-10-11 | Smart Commit の model list は #454 の modal_body と modal_list_max_h を使う。fake Ollama の request log は std Mutex の poison 回復で失敗時の証拠を保持し、試験だけの dependency は追加しない | #1080 review。固定 280px 上限は短い window の Cancel chrome を守れないため、既存 modal shell の viewport 契約を再利用する。[ADR-0044](adr/0044-smart-commit-message-generation.md#amendment--2026-10-11-keyboard-model-confirmation-1080)。 |
| 2026-10-11 | Smart Commit の空モデル集合は既存 owner-bound smart_suggest を実行し、挿入成功後だけ EN/JA の完了文を表示する。rule-based 操作は既存 generation revision を進め、以前の LLM 結果を二重適用しない | #1082。[ADR-0044 amendment](adr/0044-smart-commit-message-generation.md#amendment--2026-10-11-empty-model-fallback-1082)。生成しない empty state へ変える必要はない：同期 suggestion が開始 session を確認し、Consent も所有者を保持するため背景 tab へ誤適用せず既存 draft を置換できる。 |
| 2026-10-11 | Smart Commit の dense model picker は RowFocus／RowList と list_a11y を再利用し、一件でも Enter／Space による明示確認を必要とする。modal の開始 session 以外には生成を適用しない | #1080。[ADR-0044 amendment](adr/0044-smart-commit-message-generation.md#amendment--2026-10-11-keyboard-model-confirmation-1080)。既存 Kagi list helper が focus／scroll／AX 契約を持つため、独自 keyboard control を追加しない。 |
| 2026-10-11 | #1083 review: visibility command IDs は palette／dropdown の handle_menu_command からも GPUI platform handler へ送る。native visibility labels は palette と同じ registry label path を読む | Native GPUI action registration だけでは registry ID dispatch は動かない。JA registry は既存 Msg translation を参照し、labels の分岐を増やさない。[ADR-0085 amendment](adr/0085-unified-menu-definition.md#amendment--2026-10-11-standard-macos-application-menu-1083)。 |
| 2026-10-11 | macOS app menu に Services → Hide Kagi → Hide Others → Show All を単一 MENU_BAR から生成し、visibility は global GPUI actions から platform API へ委譲する | #1083。PID 指定の native AX probe で baseline は About／Settings／Quit のみ。issue 調査の 90b3aa0 と現行 fork pin caf5007 の OsAction は Edit 専用で Hide variant が無いため App::hide／hide_other_apps／unhide_other_apps を使う。Services は SystemMenuType::Services に委譲する。[ADR-0085](adr/0085-unified-menu-definition.md)。 |
| 2026-10-11 | Command Palette の top-aligned card geometry は `modal_shell::search_card_geometry` が所有し、scaled 16px gutter と短い window で縮む top offset から幅・残り高さを cap する。input は固定、結果のみ shrink／scroll、label は truncate＋full tooltip／AX、shortcut は shrink しない | #1084。700×500@167% の native before は card x=-69.5／幅839px で window 外。既存 modal の中央配置／compact 幅は変えない。[ADR-0029](adr/0029-command-registry-menubar.md)。 |
| 2026-10-11 | Trust Repository の save／reopen 失敗は明示的承認 modal の error state に全文を保持し、既存 modal copy helper と Retry／Cancel を再利用する。session の置換・modal の close は reopen 成功時だけ行う | #1081。承認未保存を成功に見せず、reopen 失敗で既存 session を落とさない。trust gate／klog contract は変更しない。[ADR-0160 amendment](adr/0160-git2-owner-trust-gate.md#amendment--2026-10-11-trust-failure-feedback-1081)。 |
| 2026-10-11 | #1136／#1137 review: filter 検査は Stage の add-set と Discard の index→WT checkout 対象に限定し、削除 Stage と untracked raw backup→削除を維持する。sparse=false の残存 cone/patterns と main WT の patterns は linked WT の拒否根拠にしない。skip-worktree-only と Sync filter を別 typed reason と EN/JA guidance にする | 外部 filter 不実行が問題になるのは content の変換境界だけで、raw 削除を拒否してはならない。Git が sparse を無効化した後に残す metadata を有効な sparse と誤認せず、WT ごとの設定と index bit を検査する。[ADR-0194](adr/0194-git-backend-selection.md#amendment--2026-10-11-unsupported-external-filters-1136)、[ADR-0215](adr/0215-sync-to-remote-slice1.md#amendment--2026-10-11-special-repository-refusal-1137)。 |
| 2026-10-11 | HEAD の Sync は shared read-only helper で sparse config／patterns／skip-worktree、dirty／uninitialized submodule、変更 path の current／HEAD／incoming attributes を plan と preflight で検査し、未対応 variant を typed EN/JA で拒否する。非 HEAD Sync は ref-only のため除外する | #1137。[ADR-0215 amendment](adr/0215-sync-to-remote-slice1.md#amendment--2026-10-11-special-repository-refusal-1137)。stash-shaped backup は sparse flag と nested submodule content と LFS smudge semantics の保全を証明していない。submodule ignore 設定を安全判定に使わず、incoming attributes は detached in-memory index で読んで実 index を変更しない。外部 filter command や git-lfs は起動しない。snapshot restore／conflict abort／dir-file resolution への guard 適用はこの修正の対象外。 |
| 2026-10-11 | Stage の全対象を index 編集前に attribute 解決し、named filter と LFS diff／merge marker を typed EN/JA blocker で拒否する。Discard は plan／preflight の両方で同じ検査を行う。HEAD→index の Unstage は維持する | #1136。[ADR-0194 amendment](adr/0194-git-backend-selection.md#amendment--2026-10-11-unsupported-external-filters-1136)。libgit2 は外部 clean／smudge filter を実行しないため、LFS pointer を raw worktree bytes で stage したり pointer を worktree へ checkout したりする意味論を保証できない。git-lfs や repository command を自動実行せず fresh attribute cache で info attributes と late drift も検査する。 |
| 2026-10-11 | Discard は全 backup ref を保存した後、承認済み index entry と型が異なる worktree entry 自体を unlink してから隔離 handle で force checkout する。verify は link の型・生 target bytes と stat-less private index に対する regular content を検証し、実 index は書かない | PR #1157 の Linux CI failure。libgit2 1.9.4 の empty-baseline checkout は 100644 target を ADDED／UPDATE_BLOB として REMOVE を付けず、`core.ignorecase=false` では既存 symlink を追って外部 target を truncate＋index 内容で上書きし得た。100755／LINK target は REMOVE が付く。macOS でも同設定＋旧 index と一致する link size／mtime の回帰が修正前 FAIL、修正後 PASS。fresh content hash は clean／EOL policy を維持し、一件の receipt と backup を弱めない。[ADR-0179](adr/0179-ref-backed-discard-remove-backups.md)。 |
| 2026-10-11 | #1129 review: branch config の write origin は canonical gitdir／commondir 内だけに限定し、worktreeConfig が有効なら `config.worktree` も列挙する。外部 include・symlink による範囲外 origin・対象名に一致する onbranch include は typed EN／JA の plan blocker にし、全 origin の header migration は repository 外の temporary copy で先に検証する | local include の provenance は共有ファイルへの write authority ではない。追跡ファイル／HOME config へ書込まず、HEAD 変更で消える include や Git が rename できない case-variant header を実行後 Partial にしない。下段の origin migration 決定をこの範囲で限定する。HOME／XDG_CONFIG_HOME で実際に読む global fixture と config.worktree 回帰を追加。[ADR-0053](adr/0053-branch-rename-delete-safety.md)。 |
| 2026-10-11 | Branch rename は `Reference::rename` の ref-only 経路と、repo-local origin ごとの Git `config --rename-section` を使う。plan は exact section/subsection の ordered values と include origin の SHA-256・移動キーを保持し、preflight／execute が照合、完了後に同じ ordered values を検証する | #1129。libgit2 `Branch::rename` と Kagi の prefix＋`set_str` rewrite は multivar を落とし、`foo.bar` を巻き込む。Git の parsed subsection rename は raw values／quotes／escapes を再直列化せず header だけを移し、destination 設定も消さない。global／system は列挙・書込み対象外。linked worktree も同じ local-branch 経路で全 HEAD を追従し、remote rename 専用操作は存在しない。[ADR-0053](adr/0053-branch-rename-delete-safety.md)。 |
| 2026-10-11 | Stash Push の未追跡保持件数と UntrackedExcluded warning は、同じ raw path の staged `ChangeKind::Deleted` entry を除外した共通 count を使う。実行前の verification evidence も同じ path predicate で選別する | #1140 review。`git rm --cached` は HEAD に存在する path を staged deletion と untracked の両方にするが、stash push はその path を HEAD の内容へ戻すため保持ではない。全 untracked の保持を要求した旧 verify は正しい結果にも Partial を返す。既存の通常 untracked を残し、重複 path の有無と包含／除外を porcelain-matching matrix で確認する。 |
| 2026-10-11 | Stash Push の成功予測は include_untracked=false なら除外した未追跡件数を保持として示す。共有 After detail は EN/JA の chip・AX・Copy all を一つの局所化関数で揃え、blocked plan は Current のままにする | #1140。既存の UntrackedExcluded warning はすでに残るファイルを正しく説明していたが After は常に clean だった。公開 Operation／executor には include_ignored・staged-only・keep-index option は存在しないため追加しない。staged／modified／両方／untracked-only、除外／包含の実行を Git porcelain と照合し、ignored bytes の保持も固定する。toolbar は untracked 包含固定のため native の除外 leg は実 backend plan を既存 modal へ描画し、mutation は Backend で確認する。[ADR-0176](adr/0176-app-stash-local-boundary.md)。 |
| 2026-10-11 | Stash Push の unborn HEAD は typed `StashNote::HeadRequired` で plan と preflight の両方から拒否する。blocked plan の After は Current を保持し、executor の HEAD guard は残す | #1141。Git stash は最初の commit を必要とするため、実行時まで Ready と見せない。既存 stash modal の disabled action と EN/JA refusal delivery、Backend 一件の receipt ownership を再利用する。empty／untracked／staged unborn の回帰は repository 全 file bytes 不変を要求する。[ADR-0176](adr/0176-app-stash-local-boundary.md)。 |
| 2026-10-11 | Discard の regular backup mode は Unix の owner-execute bit（0100）だけを信頼し、`core.fileMode=false`／非 Unix では既存 index mode を使う。native receipt 検証は ref 自体でなく `<ref>:file` と tree mode を照合する | #1138 review。group／other の execute bit は Git の 100755 判定ではなく、filesystem mode を無条件に採ると fileMode=false の承認状態と異なる mode を復元してしまう。legacy blob OID の保持と byte-exact 内容検証は弱めず、tree root の型を追加で検証する。 |
| 2026-10-11 | Discard の recovery root は固定名 `file` の single-entry tree とし、raw blob OID は既存の receipt／summary に維持する。entry mode は symlink 120000、regular 100644／100755。`read_backup` は旧 blob root と新 tree root の内容を返す | #1138。blob だけでは型と executable bit が失われ、`to_string_lossy` は非 UTF-8 link target を破壊する。Unix `read_link` の生 bytes を保存し、他 platform で表現できない target は破棄前に拒否する。UI／MCP／CLI に file-backup の filesystem restore consumer は存在しないため、新たな restore API は作らず、Unix の Git private-index export＋entry rename による実 round-trip を検証する。旧 backup の型は推測せず、従来の bytes recovery を保つ。[ADR-0179](adr/0179-ref-backed-discard-remove-backups.md)。 |
| 2026-10-11 | user-visible libgit2 writer も operation-independent な identity blocker を plan／preflight に持ち、signature は checkout／untracked removal 前に確定する。一方 `refs/kagi/` の snapshot／savepoint／backup は専用 `internal_signature()` による application provenance を使う | identity 未設定で recovery が失われたり、途中 checkout 後に拒否されるのを避ける。明示 snapshot も ref namespace が内部なので同じ policy。固定 `kagi <kagi@local>` は user commit の fallback ではなく internal recovery object 専用とする。下の共通 helper 方針を internal と user writer に分離。ruleset の作者 email も同じ Git parser から読む。 |
| 2026-10-11 | Commit／Amend／resolved MergeCommit の identity は実行と同じ hardened Git CLI の `git var GIT_AUTHOR_IDENT`／`GIT_COMMITTER_IDENT` で plan と preflight に確認する。user-visible libgit2 commit writer の共通 signature helper も Git の committer を読み、架空 identity への fallback を廃止する | #1154 後の hermetic Linux fixture は global identity に依存していた。環境変数・conditional config・`user.useConfigOnly` は Git 自身に解釈させ、identity が無ければ typed EN／JA blocker とする。plan は receipt を書かず、run を試みたときだけ一件記録する。conflict fixture は repo-local identity を明示する。internal recovery writer は上の専用 helper 方針に分離した。[ADR-0039](adr/0039-commit-operation-safety-policy.md)。 |
| 2026-10-10 | #1131 の backend 回帰は既存 `tests/support/isolated.rs` でテストごとに child process と専用 `KAGI_LOG_DIR` を所有する。単独 cargo 実行でも Refused 一件を検証し、whole-file hunk は born／unborn の literal 名でも Stage→Unstage と各一件の receipt を検証する | origin/dev 統合後の receipt 件数 0 は Backend の finalizer 消失ではなく、suite が外部設定した log directory に依存していたため。oplog の「tests must set KAGI_LOG_DIR」安全ガードは維持し、HOME fallback・環境変数の process-global 書換え・UI の二重記録で埋め合わせない。追加／削除 hunk は捕捉済み patch を使い、#1130 の whole-file Unstage triple と別の hunk receipt ownership を保持する。 |
| 2026-10-10 | #1131 review: Unstage の照合も表示と同じ forward HEAD→index diff で行い、照合済みの一 hunk の raw patch を反転して適用する。HEAD receipt は `Head::display()`、実行 Failed の footer/toast は `op_failed` の EN/JA wrapper を使う。file↔symlink の二 delta 表示には hunk approval/button を付けず whole-file 操作へ委ねる | 下段 #1131 の「reverse diff で両側交換照合」を置換。xdiff の LCS は逆向きで同じ分割になるとは限らず、未変更の行交換を誤拒否する。反転は raw Git path quoting・mode・EOF marker を保持し、再 diff はしない。typechange の片側表示は全体の承認ではなく、変更していないユーザーへ HunkChanged を出すより hunk 操作を提示しない。 |
| 2026-10-10 | Hunk の承認は range と raw patch の old/new 各側 SHA-256 を display read から UI → plan → preflight に渡す。context/change と EOF marker を含め、unstage は両側を交換して照合する。preflight が保持した同じ diff を index に適用し、Backend が一件の receipt を所有する | #1131。range 番号だけでは APPROVED → NOT_APPROVED の差替えを見逃す。lossy UTF-8 表示より前の bytes を使い、隣の hunk の変更を不必要に拒否せず、欠落 identity は承認できない。Untracked delta は捕捉済み patch から Added delta に変換し、worktree を再読込して stage_file に渡さない。UI は既存 HunkChanged の EN/JA 表示と拒否後 diff refresh を使う。 |
| 2026-10-10 | Commit private index は承認済み entry の stat／extended flags も複製し、MessageOnly は実 index を読んでから旧 HEAD tree を in-memory に読む。Amend に `--allow-empty` を付け、署名必須 policy は libgit2 でなく実行と同じ Git CLI で読む | #1132／#1133 review。bare tree からの index 再構築は skip-worktree を落として hook に削除と見せ、全 tracked file の再 hash を招く。empty commit の reword／HEAD diff を無くす staged amend は承認済み tree／parent に適合する。`includeIf hasconfig:remote.*.url` は Git と libgit2 で解釈が異なるため policy 照合も Git に揃える。hook fixture の signing=false と SSH signer=ssh-keygen を local に固定する。[ADR-0039](adr/0039-commit-operation-safety-policy.md)。 |
| 2026-10-10 | `commit.gpgsign` と `gpg.format` は system Git に委ね、実行直前の signing-required policy が true なら read-back HEAD の署名 header も要求する。鍵不在で unsigned commit へ fallback しない | #1133。libgit2 constructor は SSH 署名必須設定を黙殺していた。#1132 の共通 CLI executor で Commit／全 Amend mode／解決済み MergeCommit が同じ署名経路を使う。hook 後の署名欠落は Unknown、通常の鍵不在は HEAD／元 index 不変の Failed。使い捨て SSH key と allowed-signers による `git verify-commit` を backend 回帰で行う。OpenPGP／X.509 は Git の設定に従うが実鍵の native／crypto matrix は未実測。[ADR-0039 amendment](adr/0039-commit-operation-safety-policy.md#amendment--2026-10-10-users-commit-hooks-and-signing-1132-1133)。 |
| 2026-10-10 | Commit／fixup、全 Amend mode、解決済み MergeCommit は system Git の `commit` を使い、実行時に再照合した index tree から private index を作って渡す。commit だけ `core.hooksPath` の抑止を外し、完了後の HEAD tree／parents を検証する | #1132。libgit2 は hook を呼ばないため拒否 hook でも HEAD が進んだ。通常の CLI hardening は維持し、承認済み commit のみ user の hook を実行する。元 index を CLI に渡さず #1126 の外部 blob 差替え防止と message-only Amend の旧 tree を維持する。hook 失敗の stderr は一件の Failed receipt と既存 EN／JA failure footer／toast に残す。hook が tree／HEAD を変更した結果は成功とせず Unknown として要確認。[ADR-0039 amendment](adr/0039-commit-operation-safety-policy.md#amendment--2026-10-10-users-commit-hooks-and-signing-1132-1133)。 |
| 2026-10-10 | PushTag はすべての resolved push URL を plan に固定し、execute 直前にも再照合したうえで承認済み remote 名へ push する。reconcile expectation も remote 名を保持する | #1135 review。URL を直接 argv に渡すと複数 pushurl・receivepack/proxy を落とし、URL rewrite が二重に適用されうる。reconcile の reader は remote 名を要求するため URL へ置き換えない。下の単一 URL による実行方針を更新する。 |
| 2026-10-10 | PushTag の plan は remote 名・resolved push URL・raw tag object OID・peeled target を保持し、preflight は request と現状の一致を要求する。execute と remote verify は同じ承認済み URL／object を使い、bare tag name を実行 authority にしない | #1135。[ADR-0140](adr/0140-push-tag.md)。HEAD の一致だけでは同名 tag の差替えを検出できない。annotated tag の object を commit に peel して送ると annotation を失うため、explicit `<object-oid>:refs/tags/<name>` を使い、既存 remote tag の no-force refusal と一件の receipt を維持する。URL による実行は上の #1135 review 行で remote 名による実行と全 push URL の照合に更新した。 |
| 2026-10-10 | MCP commit_show の revision 解決は read-only Backend API で libgit2 revparse と tag peel を使い、graph snapshot の予算と分離する。Ambiguous／NotFound／NotACommit を typed error にし、曖昧な hex prefix の候補は object database 全体から列挙する | #1134、#1139。snapshot の先頭一致は一意性を保証せず、2000 件外の完全 SHA を誤って拒否する。object 種別・到達可能性にかかわらず prefix の一意性を libgit2 に判定させ、候補の full SHA を提示する。MCP／CLI audit で同じ snapshot scan は commit_show 以外に存在しない。 |
| 2026-10-10 | Unstage は `reset_default` の glob pathspec を廃止し、専用 `ops/unstage.rs` の plan／preflight／execute／verify で exact path の index entry を HEAD の OID／mode に戻す。plan は対象ごとの stage-0 OID／mode と conflict の有無を保持し、preflight／executor が disk index を再読込して drift を既存 typed blocker で拒否する | #1130。`reset_default` に NO_GLOB 指定 API がないため、既存 Discard／diff の literal-path 方針を exact index API で守る。POSIX の backslash を separator に変換しない。HEAD の Tree entry は file として追加せず exact path を解除し、conflict stage 1–3 を除いて検証する。bulk は一回だけ index write、途中／write 失敗は cached index を再読込して次の Stage へ漏らさない。Backend の成功 receipt は ADR-0178 の staging-family migration gap のままで ownership は変更しない。 |
| 2026-10-10 | sequence abort は Git の `rollback_is_safe()` と同じく `sequencer/abort-safety` を現在 HEAD と照合し、不一致なら typed EN/JA の理由で巻戻しを拒否する。安全基準も conflict revision に含める | #1127 review。手動 commit で CHERRY_PICK_HEAD が消えても古い sequencer が残り、後続の無関係な commit を開始点へ巻戻す危険がある。`sequencer/head` だけでは開始点の存在と「この履歴を巻戻してよい」を区別できない。基準が欠損・不正でも推測して復元しない。 |
| 2026-10-10 | cherry-pick / revert sequence の abort 原点は worktree 固有の `sequencer/head` を優先し、単一 pick は現在の HEAD を使う。sequence の開始記録が欠損・不正なら ORIG_HEAD / 現在 HEAD を推測せず拒否する。復元は既存の touched-path checkout と ref CAS、解決 buffer 退避・receipt を共有する | #1127。成功済み pick がある sequence の現在 HEAD は開始点ではなく、ORIG_HEAD は cherry-pick が更新しないため古い値になり得る。再構成する適用結果は現在 HEAD を ours とし、成功済み pick を手編集と誤判定しない。開始記録も conflict revision に含め、確認後の差替えを拒否する（[ADR-0067](adr/0067-continue-abort-skip-safety.md)）。 |
| 2026-10-10 | #1128 review: Stage の不在判定に NotADirectory も含める。一括 stage が失敗した場合は cached index を disk から強制再読込して未確定編集を破棄する | 親 directory が file に変わると旧 child は ENOTDIR となるが、Git と同じく削除を stage できる必要がある。libgit2 の Repository は index を共有するため、途中失敗した batch の編集が次の成功操作で保存されないことを回帰で固定する。 |
| 2026-10-10 | Stage の単体・一括経路は `symlink_metadata` で directory entry の存在を共有判定する。NotFound のみ削除として扱い、symlink の mode 120000 と raw target bytes は既存 libgit2 `add_path` に任せる | #1128。`Path::exists` はリンク先を追い、dangling link の更新を削除として stage していた。相対・絶対・非 UTF-8 target、新規・既存リンクを同じ backend 回帰で検証し、選択外の index identity と作業ファイルを保持する。既存 Stage の Backend／UI receipt 境界は移行しない。 |
| 2026-10-10 | Discard の absolute / `./` 互換入力だけは platform の path 解決後に Normal component を `/` で再結合する。raw repo-relative 入力は変換しない。隔離 checkout handle は `repo.path()` から開き、native 回帰は実 Discard-all click と row・path segment・badge の paint probe を観測する | #1125 independent review。Windows の normalized `PathBuf` は `\` 区切りを返すため、そのまま安全判定へ渡すと既存の入れ子 path を拒否する。Git directory からの reopen は `core.worktree`・linked worktree の identity を保つ。paint probe は実描画 child に渡す文字列から ID を作り、承認 model の一致だけを表示証拠にしない。既存の本体 checkout semantics と raw POSIX filename bytes は維持する。 |
| 2026-10-10 | Discard の repo-relative UTF-8 path bytes は UI から plan・preflight・backup・execute・verify まで変更せず、status の `Path` と照合する。別 Repository handle の in-memory index を承認済み entry だけに限定し、checkout の source と baseline を隔離して pathspec を渡さない。非 UTF-8 UI 選択・NUL・platform 上で literal に扱えない path は書込み前に拒否する | #1125。POSIX の `\` は `/` と別 identity。変換を除いても libgit2 は literal flag にかかわらず pathspec の common prefix を glob-unescape するため単体回帰が FAIL した。source だけの限定では HEAD baseline から未選択 file を削除するため、独立 handle の on-disk でない index で baseline も空にする。これにより glob/prefix 解釈なしで既存 libgit2 checkout の mode・symlink・EOL semantics を保ち、repo index と caller の cache を変更しない。absolute / `./` の既存入力は workdir 基準で解決し target symlink を canonicalize しない。承認集合と実行集合は一致を要求する。[ADR-0046](adr/0046-discard-changes.md)、[ADR-0179](adr/0179-ref-backed-discard-remove-backups.md)、[ADR-0194](adr/0194-git-backend-selection.md)。 |
| 2026-10-10 | Commit の計画結果を既存 opener と native 回帰で共有する境界に分け、typed plan refusal を `op_plan_failed` の EN／JA detail として footer／toast に出す。古い承認は解除し、実行 receipt は作らない | #1126 review。計画末尾の index 照合が拒否すると即時 Commit の旧 error branch は klog だけで「Ready」を残した。native は実 index drift の backend refusal を同じ計画結果境界へ届け、タイミング依存の planner race や偽の GitError は使わない。resolved MergeCommit は既存の承認照合をそのまま使い、MERGE_HEAD を含む無変更と一件の実行拒否 receipt を回帰で固定する。 |
| 2026-10-10 | Commit／fixup と staged／both Amend の承認は、既存 queue 用の index digest（raw path の長さと bytes・OID・mode・conflict stage の SHA-256）を共通化して plan に保持する。preflight は disk index を強制再読込し、executor は再照合した同じ in-memory index から tree を作る | #1126。HEAD／message／path／status の一致では同じパスの未承認 blob を検出できない。stat-cache は承認対象ではなく、plan で tree object や index を書かない。identity 欠落も拒否し、auto-snapshot より前に typed EN／JA 理由を返し、既存 Backend の一件の receipt に記録する。message-only Amend は旧 HEAD tree を使い、index drift を承認対象にしない。 |

| 2026-10-10 | shared fixture の `git2::Config` local-write 最適化を却下し、元の hermetic `git init` と 3 回の repo-local CLI config 設定を byte-identical に維持する。local include の実 consumer 回帰を残し、plain Git signing consumer に `maintenance.auto=false` / `gc.auto=0` の 2 key だけを追加する | Refs #1106。`Config::open` は include / includeIf を展開し、含まれる対象 key または main + include の重複 key を `set_str` が included-entry / multivar として拒否する。`~/` も fixture child の隔離 HOME ではなく parent HOME から読む。固定 git2 0.21 の安全な public API に include 非展開の file-write option はなく、`set_multivar` も読み込み時の展開を防げない。hand parser / 新依存 / unsafe FFI / fallback / error suppress で最適化を強行しない。PM の failing-before は `fixture_reinit_preserves_local_include_consumers` が `set fixture local name: entry is not unique due to being included` で実 1 FAIL。同じ test source（SHA256 `e94c6f23…`）のまま元の CLI helper（baseline と同じ `21cdec9b…`）へ戻した After は全 4 test PASS。その後 PM の `cargo fmt --all` 自動整形で test source は `c05e19c5…` となり、手編集 / assertion の再 pin はしていない。この corrected formatted source の全 7 gate は exit 0 / PASS、合計 280.224601 秒（`/tmp/kagi-hig-audit-20261008/git-fixture-include-guards-final-gates/manifest.json`）。限定 native 2 は実 KEEP_GOING 2P0F、workspace は全 4 fixture test PASS / body 1.16 秒（同 directory の `native.raw:463` / `workspace.raw:953–958`）。相対 include / `~/`、include-only / main 重複、実 libgit2 signature / commit と plain Git signing、未知 multivar / seed / included file / HOME 保持を検証した。既存 directory / gitfile / linked・hostile HOME / decoy / template / hooks・maintenance oracle も維持する。raw commit の 2 maintenance key は identity / signing の hostile-global oracle を変えず、detach と cleanup の競合を防ぐ。product / trust / write pipeline / nonzero errors / parent env / sleep / timeout / cleanup の意味は変更しない。最終変更は consumer 回帰と test isolation の補強であり、subprocess 削減や速度向上ではない。旧 prototype の full7 / v4 は最終証拠に使わず、異なる source / cache / runner の gate wall も性能比較しない。この receipt 文書更新後の fmt / invariant / default-build 再確認は PM に残り、独立外部 review の承認は別途必要。 |
| 2026-10-10 | 【撤回済みの試案：上段の同日決定で置換】shared Git fixture の `init_repo` から identity / signing の 3 subprocess を削除し、既存依存の `git2::Config` で単一 local config を更新する案を試した。`discover_path` / `commondir` で gitfile / linked worktree の保存先を解決し、helper API は維持する方針だった | Refs #1106。[ADR-0002](adr/0002-git-backend.md) に沿い、global config をロードする `Repository::open(...).config()` や新しい parser / whole-file 置換を避け、process 起動だけを削減する意図だった。include を持たない旧 3 consumer は Before / After 3/3 PASS、旧 source の全 7 gate は 374.010091 秒で PASS し、`fixture-config-measure/results-v4/manifest.json` に count / matched setup timing が残る。ただしすべて **include 境界を破る撤回済み prototype の履歴**であり、最終 source の受入れや性能改善の証拠ではない。内部 review が有効な local include の再初期化失敗と `~/` の parent HOME 読込みを指摘し、追加した実 consumer は prototype で 1 FAIL。既存 public git2 API では CLI と同じ include 非展開の書込を表現できず、上段の同日判断で元の CLI に戻した。初期計測 adapter の rustup 選択 / Python cache 問題も失敗として保持し、oracle の skip / HOME 再基準化で成功にしていない。元の採用意図と実験記録は残すが、最終変更に subprocess 削減や速度向上を帰属しない。 |

| 2026-10-09 | Linux fixture の identity 再照合 + `SIGKILL` と、identity 再照合 + exact-PID `waitpid` は fixture ごとの同じ mutex で排他する。`wait_until_gone`・poll sleep・finish / join は lock の外とし、panic 後の Drop も同じ guarded signal 経路を使う | #1094 の独立 static review は、identity 照合後・signal 前に async reaper が zombie の PID を解放できる競合を指摘した。専用 supervisor は host PID namespace を隔離しないため、照合だけではこの自己所有の reap との競合を防げない。製品コードと実 group-stop oracle は変更しない。PID reuse の実行再現を行ったという主張はしない。 |
| 2026-10-09 | Linux の process fixture はケースごとに exact-test subprocess を再実行し、その専用 process だけを `PR_SET_CHILD_SUBREAPER` にする。停止 group の外の thread が fixture の private PID + 起動 identity を照合し、その子孫 PID だけを `waitpid(WNOHANG)` で回収する。共有 harness の設定、runner の direct Child、製品の group-stop 判定は変更しない | #1094 の元の review head `8853fcff4e1b5e7e4653ce779a2f0f479c6d412a` では non-reaping Linux PID 1 に orphan zombie が残り、正しい `kill(-pgid, 0)` の存在判定によって group-stop assertion が失敗し得た。下段の「PID 1 が reap しなければ不明のまま」という fixture 制限は、この自己所有の reap で置き換える。Linux の identity は zombie も reap まで保持し、`waitpid(-1)` で別 fixture や runner の Child を奪わない。実 deadline / group-stop / 未完了 capture の oracle と macOS の動作は維持する。検証は通常 Linux と private Docker の non-reaping PID 1 の両方で行い、元 head の review 記録は残す。 |
| 2026-10-09 | process テストの後片付けは fixture が private に公開した子孫 PID と kernel の起動 identity を RAII で保持し、signal 直前に照合する。root group の panic cleanup は所有する Child が未 reap の間だけに限り、reap 後は記録した子孫 identity だけを対象にする。ホスト全体の検索・`pkill -f` と、reap 後の group 番号だけへの signal は使わない | 同じ sleep の名前や引数は利用者・並列テストの process にも一致するため。identity helper と helper を使うケースは元の Unix guard に加え macOS / Linux に限定する（macOS は既存の `tests/support/proc_identity.rs`、Linux は `/proc/<pid>/stat`）。その他 Unix で恒常的に失敗する fallback や PID だけの cleanup は作らず、既存の process-group-leadership・direct deadline・input / output テストは保持する。held-pipe 子孫が生存中の clean exit を group-stopped proof にしない。`fixture_cleanup_does_not_stop_another_same_marker_child` は同じ marker の実 sleep を 2 つ所有し、一方の cleanup / reap で他方の identity が生存することを検証する。Linux の container PID 1 が orphan zombie を reap しなければ group proof は保守的に不明のままとする。 |

| 2026-10-04 | 右ペインの開閉は `inspector_visible` の toggle ではなく Graph の解決済み `RightPane` が表示 / 非表示に変わったことを起点にする。commit のクリック・再クリック・Esc は同じ `PanelMotion` の 180/150ms と途中反転を使い、Inspector / Compare / Commit Panel の表示中の交換は動かさない。Home・タブ切替・Conflict・Graph 外への移動は即時、`reduce_motion` も即時。Esc 後の最後の Inspector 行は閉じる clip の描画だけに使い、active な選択は解除する | #1001 は下の #955 の「toggle のときだけ動く」を右ペインに限って置き換える。Graph で選択が変わっても toggle は変わらず、開閉が即時になっていた。表示中の slot 交換と workspace 全体の遷移を除外すれば、不要な二重アニメーションを増やさず selection / Esc を同じ幅の motion として扱える。Tier A `right_selection_motion` で中間幅、反転、Esc、reduced motion、tab 境界を測る。 |

| 2026-10-04 | SSH remote pull の plan job は cached branch / upstream だけでなく HEAD OID と dirty も live probe と照合し、ずれた preview を確認させない。probe は `GIT_OPTIONAL_LOCKS=0`。実行 argv は `branch.<name>.mergeOptions=`・`--no-rebase --ff --no-autostash --no-recurse-submodules` を明示して確認外の strategy / stash / submodule 更新を無効化する | #997 review。host の mergeOptions / autoStash / submodule.recurse は確認と違う結果を生み、autostash 再適用の conflict は exit 0 でも残る。preflight と実行を 1 SSH session に統合する race 対応は #1014。[ADR-0196](adr/0196-operation-lifecycle-contract.md)。 |
| 2026-10-04 | SSH remote pull は cached status の preview に background identity plan を加え、`Planned::RemotePull` → `begin_write` で `WriteScope::Remote` lease と `OperationId` / `OwnerStamp` を取る。通常の SSH options を使うので ssh-agent-only を許す。Unknown・Partial・task abandonment は lease と reconcile を保留し、停止済みでも remote の結果を観測できないため 2 段階の unobservable-release と監査記録だけで解放する。remote stash の identity-file 必須 policy は維持する | #989 PM 決定 Q1–Q4。latch と transport hold の二重の排他では quit を保留できず、remote の不明な結果にも durable な出口が無かった。[ADR-0196](adr/0196-operation-lifecycle-contract.md) / [ADR-0177](adr/0177-transport-recording-boundary.md)。agent-only の identity files は空なので再現可能性に限界がある。 |
| 2026-10-04 | snapshot の作成、conflict の continue(stash / merge 経路)、sequencer の continue 確認、conflict の skip を #992 の guard writer 型で background に移す: UI thread は owner の確認・入力の凍結・`reserve_write` まで、task は supervisor に入り `Backend::open` で実行して guard を `complete_git` / `settle_conflict_write` で決着、`fallible()` の `None` は `abandonment.into_unknown()`、完了後は busy 更新・(conflict のみ)oplog 記録・`drain_unaccounted` → reconcile 通知を常に行い、toast / footer / reload / conflict 判定 / modal error は開始時の session と visit が前面のときだけ。`[kagi]` 契約行は owner に関係なく出す。stage 後の WIP diffstat は同期の `refresh_wip_diffstat` を廃止し `start_wip_diffstat_scan` に session ごとの要求番号を足して順序付ける。restore-snapshot の遅延理由を `SlowWriteWorktree` に移す | #996(#355 R4、PM 決定 Q1〜Q6)。同期のままでは #995 の経過秒の説明が描画されない。stage / unstage / hunk は write queue(段階 3)まで同期のまま。snapshot は従来どおり oplog に記録しない。新しい `SlowRead` 種別は作らない(ADR-0206 の 5 種のまま)。同じ cache epoch 内の連続 scan は epoch だけでは順序が付かないため要求番号を足した。restore-snapshot の実体は index / worktree への checkout(#995 Codex 4176765509 への PM 返信)。 |
| 2026-10-04 | #355 段階 2 の intent queue は `src/app/queue.rs` に `Sessions` と分離して置き、lease / admission は既存の `Sessions` だけが所有する。受付は checkout・commit・merge の凍結した user input に限定し、`&&` の進行は Success・Verified・記録成功・reconcile 不要の receipt のみに従う。queue は 1 session 16 件、cancel 一覧は直近 32 件とし、auto-fetch はその session の列が空でない間 skip する | [ADR-0204](adr/0204-operation-queue.md)。段階 2 は pure reducer と unit oracle のみ。UI と適格 family の入口は段階 3 で配線し、lease 関数は変更しない。 |

| 2026-10-04 | `PlanRecovery::commands_for(ShellKind)` を唯一の出口とし、Copy all の `commands:` ブロックと入力カードの折りたたみ行は `ShellKind::current()` で取る。`ShellKind::WindowsCmd`(`cfg!(windows)`)には空を返し、説明の `plan_recovery_text` は残す | #1007。#1006 で動的 operand を POSIX の `shell_quote` で quote したが、Windows の端末の既定 shell は `cmd.exe` で、single quote も `&` の無効化も効かない。#993 の等価コマンドと同じく Windows では出さない。生成側(20 以上の plan 関数)ではなく消費側の 1 か所で絞り、unit test で `WindowsCmd` を固定する。PowerShell 向けの quote は既定 shell を設定で選べるようになってから(別 issue)。 |

| 2026-10-04 | 右クリックメニューのキーボードは共有の `menu_overlay::render_menu_overlay` に `keys: Option<&MenuKeys>` として持たせ、commit / branch(remote branch を含む)/ tag / stash / worktree のメニューだけが `Some(&app.menu_keys)` を渡す。メニューが開いたフレームで最初の有効な項目へ focus を移し、↑/↓(端で折り返し)と Home/End は有効な項目だけを移動、Enter / Space は gpui の keyboard click で項目の `on_click` を押す。閉じたフレームで、focus がメニューの中にあった(または失われた)ときだけ、開く前の focus へ戻す(項目が modal を開いたときは root、#817)。Shift+F10 と `menu`(Windows の Apps キー)は `!Terminal && !Input` で `ContextMenuKey` にする。root では #986 の `commit_list_has_focus` が真のときだけ、選択中の commit のメニューを行の左下(選択中の行だけに置いた canvas が毎フレーム記録する bounds)に開く。サイドバーでは focus のある行だけが同じ action と canvas を持ち、その行のメニュー(右クリックと同じ `open_*_menu`)を行の左下に開く。グループの見出しにはメニューが無いので何もしない | #985(PM 決定: A+B を 1 PR、サイドバーは #987 merge 後に同じ PR へ)。メニューを描く経路は 1 つなので、そこに入れれば 6 種類のメニューが同じ挙動になる。conflict のファイルメニュー・Editor のツリーメニュー・一覧の filter メニューも同じ renderer を使うが、前の 2 つは window の Escape の連鎖で閉じず、focus を取ると Escape の行き先が変わるので `None` にして従来どおりにする(gap に記載)。項目の slot は描いた(非表示でない)項目の順で、有効な項目の slot は描画のたびに `MenuKeys` 内の Vec に入れ直す(フレームごとの allocation なし)。focus の表示は `focus_visible` だけなので、ポインタで開いた場合の見た目は変わらない。サイドバーでフレームごとに行の clone と listener を払うのは focus のある 1 行だけ(フレームの始めに window の focus を 1 度読み、行の handle と比べる)。メニューを閉じると focus は開く前の行へ戻る。 |

| 2026-10-04 | Graph サイドバーの行のキーボード経路は、5 つのペインそれぞれを 1 つの Tab stop にする(`keyboard_nav::RowFocus` をペインごとに 1 つ)。↑/↓ はペインの中だけで動き、ペインをまたがない。`RowFocus` / `RowList` は `keyboard_nav::RowScroll`(`gpui::list` の `ListState` か、`uniform_list` の handle と processor が描いた範囲)を受け取る。折りたたんだペインの Tab stop は見出しで、Enter / Space で開いて最初の行へ移る(キーボードで折りたたむ経路は無い)。Enter / Space の主操作は、branch が checkout の plan(現在の branch は移動)、worktree がキーボードで開く点検カード(Esc・もう一度の Enter・focus が行を離れると閉じる)、ほかはクリックと同じ。ポインタのクリックでは行に focus を移さない(これまでどおり window が focus を持つ) | #981(PM 決定: Design 1)。各ペインは既に別々の `Role::Tree` なので、ARIA の tree と同じく 1 つの tree に 1 つの Tab stop になる。ペインをまたぐ ↑/↓ は、ペインごとに別の scroll handle と折りたたみを越える処理が要り、5 つの Tree が 1 つの Tab stop を共有することになる。`uniform_list` は item ごとの bounds を持たないが、processor が描く範囲がそのまま描画済みの範囲になる(handle の `logical_scroll_top_index` は gpui では test 専用)。点検カードは tooltip で、gpui では tooltip をキーボードから開けないので、同じ中身を行に anchored で描く。クリックで行に focus を移すと、branch をクリックしたあとの ↑/↓ が graph ではなくサイドバーを動かすようになるので、移さない。checkout の plan はルートの Enter / Escape で動く(#817)ので、行から開いたら focus をルートへ渡す。 |
